<script>
    const configs = {"coupon":{"enabled":true,"siteKey":"0x4AAAAAABSx4hvbXRZaasDq","limitCharacterCoupon":"8"},"register":{"enabled":true,"siteKey":"0x4AAAAAABSx4hvbXRZaasDq"},"enable":true};
    const initCaptchaLogin = "0" === "1" ? true : false;
    var attempts = 0;
    function _runFhsTurnstileInit () { 
        FHSRecaptcha.initRecaptcha(configs); 
        if (initCaptchaLogin){
            FHSRecaptcha.initRenderRecaptcha('login');
            fhs_account.setIsMoreFailedLogin(true);
        }
    }

    function onloadTurnstileCallback() {
        if (window.turnstile && typeof turnstile.ready === 'function'){
            // turnstile.ready(_runFhsTurnstileInit);
            this._runFhsTurnstileInit();
            return;
        }

        // retry 10 time
        var maxAttempts = 10; // ~2s
        var poll = setInterval(function(){
            attempts++;
            if (window.turnstile && typeof turnstile.ready === 'function'){
                clearInterval(poll);
                // turnstile.ready(_runFhsTurnstileInit);
                this._runFhsTurnstileInit();
            } else if (attempts >= maxAttempts){
                clearInterval(poll);
                console.error('turnstile not available after waiting');
            }
        }, 100);
    }
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    
    $jq('.catalog_menu_dropdown .verticalmenu > li > a').removeClass('dropdown-toggle');
    $jq('.catalog_menu_dropdown .verticalmenu > li > a').removeAttr('data-toggle');
    
    $jq('.catalog_menu_dropdown .verticalmenu > li > a').hover(function(){
	$jq('.catalog_menu_dropdown .verticalmenu > li').removeClass('active');
	$jq(this).parent().addClass('active');
	showMenuContent();
    });
    showMenuContent();
    function showMenuContent(){
	let $item_active = $jq('.catalog_menu_dropdown .verticalmenu > li.active');
	if($item_active.length <= 0){
	    $item_active = $jq('.catalog_menu_dropdown .verticalmenu > li').first();
	    $item_active.addClass('active');
	}else{
	    $item_active = $item_active.first();
	}

	if($item_active.length > 0){
	    let $dropdown = $item_active.find('.dropdown-menu-inner');
	    let $title = $item_active.children('a');
	    $jq('.fhs_header_desktop .fhs_menu_title').html($title.html());
	    $jq('.fhs_header_desktop .fhs_menu_content').html($dropdown.html());
	}
    }
</script>                </div>

</script>

$jq(document).ready(function () {
	if ($jq('#search_mobile').val() == "" || typeof $jq('#search_mobile').val() === 'undefined') {
		$jq('.remove-text-search-form-mini-mobile').hide();
	} else {
		$jq('.remove-text-search-form-mini-mobile').show();
	}
});

$jq('#search_mobile').on("keyup", function () {
	if ($jq('#search_mobile').val() != "") {
		$jq('.remove-text-search-form-mini-mobile').show();
	} else {
		$jq('.remove-text-search-form-mini-mobile').hide();
	}
});
$jq('.remove-text-search-form-mini-mobile').click(function () {
	$jq('#search_mobile').val("");
        $jq('#search_mobile').focus();
	$jq('.remove-text-search-form-mini-mobile').hide();
});

<script type="text/javascript">
    
    const CUSTOMER_NOTIFICATION_URL = "https://www.fahasa.com/customer/notification" ;
    const TOP_NOTIFICATION_URL = "/node_api/user/top_notifications";
    const COUNT_TOP_NOTIFICATION_URL = "/node_api/user/count_top_notifications";
    
    const NOTIFICATION_URL = "https://cdn1.fahasa.com/skin/frontend/ma_vanese/fahasa/images/customer/";
    
    var IS_LOAD_DATA_NOTI = false;
    var IS_LOAD_INFO = false;
    
   $top_notification_menu = $jq(".top-notification-menu");
   $top_notification_loading = $jq(".top-notification-menu .top-notification-loading");
   $top_notification_view_all = $jq(".top-notification-menu .top-notification-view-all");
   $top_notification_no_msgs = $jq(".top-notification-menu .top-notification-no-messages");
   $top_notification_list = $jq(".top-notification-menu .top-notification-list");
   $top_notification_list.empty();
    
    $jq(window).on('notification_button_loaded', function(){
        $jq("#top-notification-button-show").empty();
        
        let html = '<div class="top-notification-button">'
        + '<a style="cursor:pointer;display:flex;flex-direction: column;position: relative;">'
        +  '<div class="fhs_center_center" style="margin-left: 4px;margin-right: 4px;">'
        +    '<div class="icon_nofi_white">'
	+	'<div>'
	+	    '<div class="top-notification-button-unseen top_menu_unseen fhs_center_center" style="display: none;"></div>'
	+	'</div>'
	+    '</div>'
        + '</div>'
        +  '</a>'
        + '<div class="top-notification-menu fhs_dropdown">'
	+ '<div style="padding:16px;border-bottom: 1px solid #F2F4F5; font-weight: bold;" class="fhs_center_space">'
	+   '<div class="fhs_center_left" style="font-size: 1.23em; color: #0D0E0F;">'
		+ '<span class="icon_nofi_black" style="width:16px;height:16px;margin-right: 8px;"></span>'
		+ '<span>Thông báo</span>'
		+ '<span class="fhs_notification_unseen" style="margin-left:4px;"></span>'
	+ '</div>'
	+ '<a href="/customer/notification" class="fhs_center_left" style="color: #2489F4; font-size: 1.1em;"> Xem tất cả</a>'
        + '</div>'
	+ '<div class="top-notification-loading fhs_center_center" style="padding:16px;">'
        + '<div class="ico_loading_cirle"></div>'
        + '</div>'
        + '<div class="top-notification-list"></div>'
        + '<div class="top-notification-footer"></div>'
        + '</div>'
        + '</div>';
	
	if(fhs_account && !fhs_account.isEmpty(fhs_account.myInfo)){
            let myInfo = fhs_account.myInfo;
            if(myInfo.islogin){
               $jq("#top-notification-button-show").append(html); 
               
        $jq(".fhs_header_mobile .top-notification-button a").click(function () {
	if(!$top_notification_menu.is(":visible")){
            //$top_notification_menu.show();
	    fhs_account.showDropDown($jq(this).parent());
	    fhs_account.showTopCover($jq(this).parent());
            if(!IS_LOAD_DATA_NOTI){
               IS_LOAD_DATA_NOTI = true;
               if(IS_LOAD_INFO && !fhs_account.isEmpty(fhs_account.myInfo)) {
                        let info = fhs_account.myInfo;
                        if (myInfo.islogin) {
                            loadNoticationTop(info);
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                fhs_account.hideTopCover($jq(this).parent());
            }
    });
            }
	}
    });
    
    $jq(window).on('notification_loaded', function(){
	if(fhs_account && !fhs_account.isEmpty(fhs_account.myInfo)){
	    IS_LOAD_INFO = true;
            let myInfo = fhs_account.myInfo;
            if(myInfo.islogin){
                loadCountNoticationTop(myInfo);
            }
            
            // init again:
            $top_notification_menu = $jq(".top-notification-menu");
            $top_notification_loading = $jq(".top-notification-menu .top-notification-loading");
            $top_notification_view_all = $jq(".top-notification-menu .top-notification-view-all");
            $top_notification_no_msgs = $jq(".top-notification-menu .top-notification-no-messages");
            $top_notification_list = $jq(".top-notification-menu .top-notification-list");
            $top_notification_list.empty();
	}
    });
    
    const NOTIFICATION_ICONS = {
        'order': 'notification_order.png',
        'event': 'notification_event.png',
        'coupon': 'notification_coupon.png',
        'action': 'notification_action.png'
    }
   
   
    
    $jq(window).click(function(e) {
	$target = $jq(e.target);
	if($target.parents('.noti-top').length == 0 && $target.parents('.top-notification-button').length == 0){
	    $top_notification_menu.hide();
	    if(is_in_search_form == false){
		fhs_account.hideTopCover(null);
	    }
	}
    });
    
    $jq('.fhs_header_desktop .noti-top').hover(
	function(){
            $top_notification_menu.show();
            if(!IS_LOAD_DATA_NOTI){
               IS_LOAD_DATA_NOTI = true;
               if(IS_LOAD_INFO && !fhs_account.isEmpty(fhs_account.myInfo)){
                   let info = fhs_account.myInfo
                   loadNoticationTop(info);
               }
            }
	},
	function(){
            $top_notification_menu.hide();
	}
    );
       
    function loadNoticationTop(info){
        let session_id = info.session_id;
        let customer_sess = info.customer_id;
	$jq.ajax({
	    url: TOP_NOTIFICATION_URL,
	    method: 'post',
	    data: {
		session_id: session_id,
                customer_sess : customer_sess,
	    },
	    success: function (result) {
		$top_notification_loading.hide();
		// ket qua result tra ve true nhung data null 
		if (!result.success || result.data.length <= 0) {
		    $top_notification_no_msgs.show();
		    return;
		}
//
//		$unseen_number = $jq(".top-notification-button-unseen");
//
//		$unseen_number.attr('data', result['unseen_cnt']);
//		if(result['unseen_cnt'] > 0){
//		    $jq('.fhs_notification_unseen').text('('+result['unseen_cnt']+")");
//		    if(result['unseen_cnt'] > 99){
//			result['unseen_cnt'] = '99+';
//		    }
//		    $unseen_number.text(result['unseen_cnt']);
//		    $unseen_number.show();
//		}
		
		$top_notification_list.empty();
		for(let i=0; i < result['data'].length; i ++){
		    $msg = addTopNotification(result['data'][i]);
		    $top_notification_list.append($msg);
		}
		
		$top_notification_list.show();
	    }
	});
    }
    
    function loadCountNoticationTop(info){
        let session_id = info.session_id;
        let customer_sess = info.customer_id;
        let is_login = info.islogin;
	$jq.ajax({
	    url: COUNT_TOP_NOTIFICATION_URL,
	    method: 'post',
	    data: {
		session_id: session_id,
                customer_sess : customer_sess,
                is_login : is_login
	    },
	    success: function (result) {
                $unseen_number = $jq(".top-notification-button-unseen");
                if(result['success']){
                    $unseen_number.attr('data', result['number']);
                    if(result['number'] > 0){
                        $jq('.fhs_notification_unseen').text('('+result['number']+")");
                        if(result['number'] > 99){
                            result['number'] = '99+';
                        }
                        $unseen_number.text(result['number']);
                        $unseen_number.show();
                    }
                }
		
	    }
	});
    }
    
    function addTopNotification(item){
        let url = "/customer/notification";
	if(item['url']){
	    url = item['url'];
	}
	let icon_active = '';
	if(item['seen_status'] == "0"){
	    icon_active = '<i class="ico_dot_red"></i>';
	}
	let icon = 'ico_noti_other';
        if(item['page_type']){
	    switch(item['page_type']){
		case 'fpoint':
		    icon = 'ico_noti_fpoint';
		break;
		case 'coupon':
		case 'event':
		    icon = 'ico_noti_promotion';
		break;
		case 'order':
		    icon = 'ico_noti_bill';
		break;
	    }
        }
        
        let item_html =  "<a class='top-notification-item fhs_column_left fhs_mouse_point clear' onclick='setSeenNoti($jq(this), "+item['id']+");' href='"+url+"'>"
			    +"<div class='fhs_top_stretch' style='width:100%;'>"
				+"<div class='fhs_top_left' style='margin-right:12px;'><div class='"+icon+"'>"+icon_active+"</div></div>"
				+"<div class='fhs_column_stretch' style='width:calc(100% - 44px); text-align: left;'>"
				    +"<div class='fhs_top_left fhs_nowrap_one' style='font-weight:bold;'>"+item['title']+"</div>"
				    +"<div class='fhs_top_left fhs_nowrap_two'>" + item['content'] + "</div>"
				    +"<div style='color:#7A7E7F;'>" + item['created_at'] + "</div>"
				+"</div>"
			    +"</div>"
			+"</a>";
        
        return item_html;
    }
    
    function setSeenNoti($item, id){
        $jq.ajax({
            url: "/customer/notification/clearunseen",
            method: 'post',
            data: {
                type: 'seen',
                msg_id: id,
            },
            success: function (data) {
//                $item.removeClass('notification-item-unseen');
//                $unseen_number = $jq(".top-notification-button-unseen");
//		let unseen = $unseen_number.attr('data');
//		if(unseen > 1){
//		    unseen = unseen -1;
//		    $jq('.fhs_notification_unseen').text('('+unseen+")");
//		    $unseen_number.attr('data'. unseen);
//		    if(unseen > 99){
//			unseen = '99+';
//		    }
//		    $unseen_number.text(unseen);
//		}else{
//		    $unseen_number.hide();
//		}
		
            }
        });
    }    
//    loadNoticationTop();
</script>

        <script type="text/javascript">       
	    function resizeHeightImg(){
		let cW = $jq('.inivoslider .swiper-wrapper').width();
		let aW = $jq('.inivoslider .swiper-wrapper').attr('width');
		let aH = $jq('.inivoslider .swiper-wrapper').attr('height');
		let img_height = Math.round((cW/aW) * aH);
		$jq('.inivoslider .swiper-wrapper').css("height", img_height);
	    }
	    resizeHeightImg();
	    $jq(window).on('resize', function(){
		resizeHeightImg();
	    });
            var inivoslider;
            $jq(document).ready(function(){
		inivoslider = new Swiper('.inivoslider', {
		    autoplay: {
			delay: 3000,
		    },
		    loop: true,
		    preloadImages: false,
		    lazy: true,
		    navigation: {
		      nextEl: '.swiper-button2-next',
		      prevEl: '.swiper-button2-prev',
		    },
		    pagination: {
			el: ".swiper-pagination",
		    },
		});
		
		if(!isMobile()){
		    $jq('.inivoslider').hover(function(){
			inivoslider.autoplay.stop();
			//$jq('.swiper-button').fadeIn(100);
		    },
		    function(){
			inivoslider.autoplay.start();
			//$jq('.swiper-button').fadeOut(100);
		    });
		}
            });    
	    function isMobile() {
		try{ document.createEvent("TouchEvent"); return true; }
		catch(e){ return false; }
	    }
        </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
var search_history_data = {};

$jq(".button-search").click(
    function() {
        $jq("form#search_mini_form_desktop").submit();
    });

$jq(window).on('myInfo_loaded', function() {
    if (fhs_account.isLoggined()) {
        $jq('.fhs-header-top-bar').addClass('logined');
    }
});

$jq(document).on('scroll', function() {
    calHeader();
});

function calHeader() {
    var point = getCurrentScroll();
    if (point >= 28) {
        $jq(".fhs_header_mobile .fhs-header-top-bar").addClass('holded');
        $jq(".toolbar-header").addClass('toolbar-box-shadow');
    } else {
        $jq(".fhs_header_mobile .fhs-header-top-bar").removeClass('holded');
        $jq(".toolbar-header").removeClass('toolbar-box-shadow');
    }
}

function getCurrentScroll() {
    return window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop;
}

calHeader();

var is_in_search_form = false;
var is_focus_search_form = false;
var is_search_loaded = false;
var key_hot_data = {};
var key_hot_page = 1;
var key_hot_limit = 6;
var cat_hot_data = {};
var cat_hot_page = 1;
var cat_hot_limit = 8;

$jq('.input-search').focus(function(e) {
    if (!is_search_loaded) {
        getKeywords(this);
    }

    if (!fhs_account.isEmpty($jq('.form-search-form').html())) {
        if (!$jq('.form-search-form').is(':visible')) {

            fhs_account.showTopCover($jq(this).parent());
            $jq('.form-search-form').fadeIn(0);
        }
    }
    is_focus_search_form = true;
}).blur(function() {
    if (!is_in_search_form) {
        $jq('.form-search-form').fadeOut(0);
        fhs_account.hideTopCover($jq(this).parent());
    }
});

$jq('.input-search').click(function() {
    if (!fhs_account.isEmpty($jq('.form-search-form').html())) {
        fhs_account.hideAllDropDown();

        fhs_account.showTopCover($jq(this).parent());
        $jq('.form-search-form').fadeIn(0);
    } else {
        fhs_account.hideAllDropDown();
    }
    is_focus_search_form = true;
});
$jq('.form-search').hover(
    function() {
        is_in_search_form = true;
    },
    function() {
        is_in_search_form = false;
    }
);

function removeSearchHistoryAll() {
    $jq('.form_search_history').remove();
    if ($jq('.form-search-form .fhs_top_account_line').length > 0) {
        $jq('.form-search-form .fhs_top_account_line').fadeOut(0);
    }

    if (fhs_account.isEmpty($jq('.form-search-form').html())) {
        $jq('.form-search-form').fadeOut(0);
        $jq('.fhs_dropdown_cover').remove();
    }
    $jq.ajax({
        url: '/productviewed/index/removeSeachHistoryAll',
        method: 'post',
        dataType: "json",
        contentType: 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8',
        success: function(data) {
            if (data['success']) {}
        }
    });
}

function removeSearchHistory(e) {
    let search_history = '';
    let search_history_index = $jq(e).attr('data');
    if (search_history_data) {
        if (search_history_data.length > 0) {
            search_history = search_history_data[search_history_index];

            $jq(e).parent().remove();

            if (fhs_account.isEmpty($jq('.form-search-form-content').html())) {
                if ($jq('.form-search-form .fhs_top_account_line').length > 0) {
                    $jq('.form-search-form .fhs_top_account_line').fadeOut(0);
                }
                $jq('.form_search_history').remove();
            }

            if (fhs_account.isEmpty($jq('.form-search-form').html())) {
                $jq('.form-search-form').fadeOut(0);
                $jq('.fhs_dropdown_cover').remove();
            }

            $jq.ajax({
                url: '/productviewed/index/removeSeachHistory',
                method: 'post',
                data: {
                    search_key: search_history
                },
                dataType: "json",
                contentType: 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8',
                success: function(data) {
                    if (data['success']) {}
                }
            });
        }
    }
}

function refreshKeyHot() {
    let items = key_hot_data.slice(((key_hot_page - 1) * key_hot_limit), (key_hot_page * key_hot_limit));
    if (items.length > 0 && items.length < key_hot_limit && key_hot_data.length >= key_hot_limit) {
        let start = key_hot_data.length - key_hot_limit;
        items = key_hot_data.slice(start, key_hot_data.length);
    } else if (items.length <= 0) {
        key_hot_page = 1;
        items = key_hot_data.slice(((key_hot_page - 1) * key_hot_limit), (key_hot_page * key_hot_limit));
    }
    let items_html = '';
    Object.keys(items).forEach(function(key) {
        let item = items[key];
        let mark = '?';
        if (item['url'].indexOf("?") != -1) {
            mark = "&";
        }
        items_html += "<a class='fhs_stretch_stretch border_radius_normal padding_small' href='" + item['url'] +
            mark + "fhs_campaign=POPULAR_SEARCH'><img class='lazyload' src='" + loading_icon_url +
            "' data-src='" + item['image_src'] +
            "' /><div class='content_text fhs_nowrap_two padding_left_normal'>" + item['value'] + "</div></a>";
    });
    $jq('.form_search_form_keyhot_content').html(items_html);
    key_hot_page++;
}

function refreshCatHot() {
    let items = cat_hot_data.slice(((cat_hot_page - 1) * cat_hot_limit), (cat_hot_page * cat_hot_limit));
    if (items.length > 0 && items.length < cat_hot_limit && cat_hot_data.length >= cat_hot_limit) {
        let start = cat_hot_data.length - cat_hot_limit;
        items = cat_hot_data.slice(start, cat_hot_data.length);
    } else if (items.length <= 0) {
        cat_hot_page = 1;
        items = cat_hot_data.slice(((cat_hot_page - 1) * cat_hot_limit), (cat_hot_page * cat_hot_limit));
    }

    let items_html = '';
    Object.keys(items).forEach(function(key) {
        let item = items[key];
        let mark = '?';
        if (item['url'].indexOf("?") != -1) {
            mark = "&";
        }
        items_html += "<a class='fhs_column_center border_radius_normal padding_small' href='" + item['url'] +
            mark + "fhs_campaign=POPULAR_SEARCH'><img class='lazyload' src='" + loading_icon_url +
            "' data-src='" + item['image_src'] +
            "' /><div class='content_text  padding_top_normal'><span class='fhs_nowrap_two'>" + item['value'] +
            "</span></div></a>";
    });
    $jq('.form_search_form_cathot_content').html(items_html);
    cat_hot_page++;
}

function getKeywords(e) {
    is_search_loaded = true;
    $jq.ajax({
        url: '/productviewed/index/getKeyword',
        method: 'post',
        dataType: "json",
        contentType: 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8',
        success: function(data) {
            if (data['success']) {
                let data_form = '';
                let history = '';
                let keyword = '';
                let cat_hot = '';
                let banner_hot = '';
                let i = 0;

                search_history_data = data['history'];
                key_hot_data = data['keyword'];
                cat_hot_data = data['cat_hot'];

                if (data['banner_hot']) {
                    Object.keys(data['banner_hot']).forEach(function(key) {
                        let item = data['banner_hot'][key];
                        let background_color = '';
                        let text_color = '';
                        if (item['background_color']) {
                            background_color = "background-color:" + item['background_color'] + ";";
                        }
                        if (item['text_color']) {
                            text_color = "color:" + item['text_color'] + ";";
                        }
                        let mark = '?';
                        if (item['url'].indexOf("?") != -1) {
                            mark = "&";
                        }
                        banner_hot += "<a href='" + item['url'] + mark +
                            "fhs_campaign=POPULAR_SEARCH' class='swiper-slide fhs_center_space border_radius_normal' style='" +
                            background_color + " " + text_color + "'><span>" + fhs_account
                            .encodeHTML(item['value']) + "</span><img class='lazyload' src='" +
                            loading_icon_url + "' data-src='" + item['image_src'] + "' /></a>";
                    });
                }

                if (!fhs_account.isEmpty(banner_hot)) {
                    data_form = "<div class='form-search-form-banner'><div class='swiper-wrapper'>" +
                        banner_hot + "</div></div>";
                }

                if (data['history']) {
                    Object.keys(data['history']).forEach(function(key) {
                        let item = data['history'][key];
                        history += "<div style='max-width:100%'><span class='form_search_history_text'>" + fhs_account
                            .encodeHTML(item) + "</span><i class='icon_close_gray' data='" + i +
                            "' onclick='removeSearchHistory(this);event.stopPropagation();'/></div>";
                        i++;
                    });
                }
                if (!fhs_account.isEmpty(history)) {
                    let history_title =
                        '<div class="form-search-form-title fhs_center_space"><div class="fhs_center_left"><span class="fhs_center_left"><img src="https://cdn1.fahasa.com/skin/frontend/ma_vanese/fahasa/images/ico_searchhistory_black.svg"/></span><span>Lịch sử tìm kiếm</span></div><div class="fhs_center_right fhs_mouse_point" onclick="removeSearchHistoryAll();">Xóa tất cả</div></div>';
                    let history_content = '<div class="form-search-form-content form_search_history">' +
                        history + '</div>';
                    history = history_title + history_content;
                }

                if (data['keyword']) {
                    let key_hot_data_page = data['keyword'].slice(((key_hot_page - 1) * key_hot_limit), (
                        key_hot_page * key_hot_limit));
                    Object.keys(key_hot_data_page).forEach(function(key) {
                        let item = key_hot_data_page[key];
                        let mark = '?';
                        if (item['url'].indexOf("?") != -1) {
                            mark = "&";
                        }
                        keyword +=
                            "<a class='fhs_stretch_stretch border_radius_normal padding_small' href='" +
                            item['url'] + mark +
                            "fhs_campaign=POPULAR_SEARCH'><img class='lazyload' src='" +
                            loading_icon_url + "' data-src='" + item['image_src'] +
                            "' /><div class='content_text fhs_nowrap_two padding_left_normal'>" +
                            fhs_account.encodeHTML(item['value']) + "</div></a>";
                        i++;
                    });
                }
                if (!fhs_account.isEmpty(keyword)) {
                    let keyword_title =
                        '<div class="form-search-form-title fhs_center_space"><div class="fhs_center_left"><span class="fhs_center_left"><img src="https://cdn1.fahasa.com/skin/frontend/ma_vanese/fahasa/images/ico_searchtrending_black.svg"/></span><span>Từ khóa hot</span></div><div class="fhs_center_right fhs_mouse_point" onclick="refreshKeyHot();"><span class="ico_refresh_gray"></span></div></div>';
                    let keyword_content = '<div class="form_search_form_keyhot_content">' + keyword +
                        '</div>';
                    keyword = keyword_title + keyword_content;
                    key_hot_page++;
                }

                if (!fhs_account.isEmpty(history) && !fhs_account.isEmpty(keyword)) {
                    data_form += history + "<div class='fhs_top_account_line'></div>" + keyword;
                } else {
                    data_form += history + keyword;
                }

                if (data['cat_hot']) {
                    let cat_hot_data_page = data['cat_hot'].slice(((cat_hot_page - 1) * cat_hot_limit), (
                        cat_hot_page * cat_hot_limit));
                    Object.keys(cat_hot_data_page).forEach(function(key) {
                        let item = cat_hot_data_page[key];
                        let mark = '?';
                        if (item['url'].indexOf("?") != -1) {
                            mark = "&";
                        }
                        cat_hot +=
                            "<a class='fhs_column_center border_radius_normal padding_small' href='" +
                            item['url'] + mark +
                            "fhs_campaign=POPULAR_SEARCH'><img class='lazyload' src='" +
                            loading_icon_url + "' data-src='" + item['image_src'] +
                            "' /><div class='content_text  padding_top_normal'><span class='fhs_nowrap_two'>" +
                            fhs_account.encodeHTML(item['value']) + "</span></div></a>";
                    });
                }
                if (!fhs_account.isEmpty(cat_hot)) {
                    let cat_hot_title =
                        '<div class="form-search-form-title fhs_center_space"><div class="fhs_center_left"><span class="fhs_center_left"><img width = "20" height = "20" src="https://cdn1.fahasa.com/skin/frontend/ma_vanese/fahasa/images/ico_menu.svg"/></span><span>Danh mục nổi bật</span></div><div class="fhs_center_right fhs_mouse_point" onclick="refreshCatHot();"><span class="ico_refresh_gray"></span></div></div>';
                    let cat_hot_content = '<div class="form_search_form_cathot_content">' + cat_hot +
                        '</div>';
                    cat_hot = cat_hot_title + cat_hot_content;
                    cat_hot_page++;
                }

                if (!fhs_account.isEmpty(data_form) && !fhs_account.isEmpty(cat_hot)) {
                    data_form = data_form + "<div class='fhs_top_account_line'></div>" + cat_hot;
                }

                if (!fhs_account.isEmpty(data_form)) {
                    $jq('.form-search-form').html(data_form);
                    $jq('.form-search-form-content.form_search_history span').click(function() {
                        let keyw = $jq(this).text();
                        $jq('.input-search').val(keyw);
                        $jq('.form-search').append(
                            '<input type="hidden" name="fhs_campaign" value="SEARCH_HISTORY">');
                        $jq(".button-search").click();
                    });
                    if (is_focus_search_form) {
                        $jq('.form-search-form').fadeIn(0);
                        fhs_account.showTopCover($jq(e).parent());
                    }
                    if (!fhs_account.isEmpty(banner_hot)) {
                        new Swiper($jq(".form-search-form-banner"), {
                            slidesPerView: 1,
                            loop: true,
                            direction: 'horizontal',
                            simulateTouch: true,
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    });
}
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener('touchend', function(e) {
    if (e.target.closest('.fhs-tab-btn')) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (document.activeElement) {
            document.activeElement.blur();
        }
    }
}, { passive: true });
</script>

	$jq('.fhs-asidebar-tab-items li').click(function(){
	    if($jq(this).hasClass('productviewed')){
		if($jq(".fhs-asidebar").hasClass('active') && $jq(".fhs-asidebar").hasClass('productviewed')){
		    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").removeClass('active');
		}else{
		    removeAllAsidebarActive();
		    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").addClass('active');
		    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").addClass('productviewed');
		    $jq('.fhs-asidebar-content-item-bottom a').attr('href','/productviewed');
		    
		    product_viewed_asidebar.initAsizebar();
		}
	    }else if($jq(this).hasClass('suggestion')){
		if($jq(".fhs-asidebar").hasClass('active') && $jq(".fhs-asidebar").hasClass('suggestion')){
		    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").removeClass('active');
		}else{
		    removeAllAsidebarActive();
		    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").addClass('active');
		    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").addClass('suggestion');
		    $jq('.fhs-asidebar-content-item-bottom a').attr('href','/daily-suggestion');
		    
		    personalization.initAsizebar();
		}
	    }else if($jq(this).hasClass('seriesbook')){
		if($jq(".fhs-asidebar").hasClass('active') && $jq(".fhs-asidebar").hasClass('seriesbook')){
		    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").removeClass('active');
		}else{
		    removeAllAsidebarActive();
		    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").addClass('active');
		    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").addClass('seriesbook');
		    if(fhs_account.isLoggined()){
			$jq('.fhs-asidebar-content-item-bottom a').attr('href','/tryout/seriesbook/');
		    }else{
			$jq('.fhs-asidebar-content-item-bottom a').attr('href','/all-series.html');
		    }
		    
		    seriesbook_page.initAsizebar();
		}
	    }
	});
	function removeAllAsidebarActive(){
	    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").removeClass('productviewed');
	    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").removeClass('suggestion');
	    $jq(".fhs-asidebar").removeClass('seriesbook');
	}
	$jq(document).on('click', function (e) {
	    if ($jq(e.target).closest(".fhs-asidebar").length === 0) {
		$jq(".fhs-asidebar").removeClass('active');
	    }
	});
    </script>
